Build a polished, production-quality, mobile-first PWA prototype for:

VAISHNAVI HOUSEKEEPING SERVICES

This is NOT a landing page and NOT a static UI mockup.

Build a fully interactive demo of what a real Vaishnavi service marketplace/platform could look like.

URBAN COMPANY × RAPIDO × ZOMATO

but designed specifically around Vaishnavi's brand and services.

The entire demo must run locally with mock data and a persistent local database. No real backend, authentication, payments, GPS, or external APIs are required.

The goal is that I can open this on my phone, add it to my home screen, and demonstrate it to clients tomorrow as if it were a real application.

==================================================

BRAND ==================================================

Use the provided Vaishnavi logo/branding assets wherever appropriate.

Do NOT create a generic SaaS dashboard.

Do NOT make it look like Canva.

Do NOT use excessive gradients, neon colors, random decorative shapes, or unnecessary icons.

The app should feel like a serious Indian startup/product.

================================================== 2. CORE CONCEPT
There are THREE different roles inside the same prototype:

CUSTOMER
WORKER
VAISHNAVI ADMIN / OPERATIONS
All three roles must use the SAME underlying local database.

This is extremely important.

If a customer creates a booking:

CUSTOMER ↓ booking created in local DB ↓ WORKER receives request ↓ worker accepts ↓ booking updates ↓ CUSTOMER sees worker assigned ↓ worker starts trip ↓ customer sees live tracking ↓ worker arrives ↓ worker starts service ↓ worker completes service ↓ customer pays ↓ customer reviews ↓ ADMIN sees the entire process

The state must remain synchronized between all three roles.

================================================== 3. LOCAL DATABASE / PERSISTENCE
Use a real browser-persistent local database.

Prefer:

IndexedDB + Dexie

or an equally reliable local persistence solution.

DO NOT rely only on React state or local component state.

Persist:

users workers services bookings bookingEvents messages notifications payments reviews addresses ads workerEarnings adminData

If I:

Create a booking
Switch accounts
Close the browser
Reopen the application
the booking must still exist.

Seed the database with realistic mock data on first launch.

Provide a database initialization/seed mechanism that doesn't duplicate records every time the app starts.

Use:

================================================== 40. IMPORTANT STATE ARCHITECTURE
There must be ONE source of truth.

Example:

Booking status is stored in the database.

Do not separately fake the status on customer and worker screens.

If worker accepts booking:

database changes to ASSIGNED

Customer reads ASSIGNED.

Worker reads ASSIGNED.

Admin reads ASSIGNED.

If worker starts trip:

database changes to ON_THE_WAY.

All three roles reflect that.

If worker completes:

database changes to COMPLETED.

Customer sees completion.

Worker sees completed job.

Admin sees completed booking.

This shared state is what makes the demo convincing.

================================================== 41. UX DETAILS
Always make the next action obvious.

Examples:

BOOK SERVICE CONTINUE CONFIRM BOOKING TRACK WORKER CALL MESSAGE START TRIP START SERVICE COMPLETE SERVICE PAY ₹650 RATE SERVICE

Avoid dead buttons.

If a button isn't implemented, don't show it.

Everything visible should either work or open a meaningful demo interaction.

SIGNATURE MOTION + UI LANGUAGE ==================================================
The interface must have a distinctive modern "Suhaeb-style" motion language.

Do NOT use generic corporate SaaS animations.

The UI should feel tactile, playful and physical while remaining premium and professional.

MORPHING UI:

Cards, buttons, sheets and floating elements should feel slightly organic rather than rigid.

Use subtle morphing between states:

cards expand smoothly into detail views
buttons can stretch slightly when pressed
bottom sheets have soft spring movement
selected cards smoothly grow/reorganize
notification bubbles can morph into full notification cards
booking status elements can transform from one state into another
Think physical UI objects rather than simple CSS fades.

BOUNCY / STRETCHY MOTION:

Use spring-based Framer Motion animations.

Buttons:

On press: scale slightly down → stretch/compress → spring back.

Cards:

On tap: small scale compression → expand into the next state.

Bottom navigation:

Selected item should have a subtle elastic/morphing transition.

Notifications:

New notification bubbles should enter with a slight overshoot, then settle naturally.

Worker marker:

Use a soft pulse and subtle spring movement rather than a rigid blinking animation.

MAP:

When the worker moves:

animate the marker smoothly along the route.

When the worker reaches a new state:

the tracking card should subtly expand/morph.

ACCOUNT SWITCHING:

When switching between Customer / Worker / Admin:

use a polished shared transition.

The current interface should slide/morph into the new role rather than abruptly changing.

Do NOT make animations cartoonish.

The feeling should be:

"premium physical interface"

not:

"children's app."

Use spring physics intelligently.

Preferred animation characteristics:

spring-based transitions
slight overshoot
200–500ms interaction response
smooth ease-out for ordinary transitions
subtle scale transforms
opacity + translate combinations
layout animations
shared-layout transitions where useful
Respect prefers-reduced-motion.

================================================== 48. BRANDING CONSISTENCY
The app must visually belong to VAISHNAVI HOUSEKEEPING SERVICES.

Use:

VAISHNAVI

as the main brand treatment where appropriate.

================================================== 49. GLASS / MORPHISM
Use morphism/glass effects selectively.

Do NOT turn the entire interface into translucent glass.

Use subtle translucent surfaces for:

map floating controls
tracking bottom sheets
notification overlays
account switcher
floating action elements
certain modal surfaces
Use:

backdrop-filter blur semi-transparent cream/white surfaces soft borders very subtle shadows

The main application should remain solid, readable and premium.

Avoid the overused "everything is glass" aesthetic.

================================================== 50. MICRO-INTERACTIONS
Every major interaction should have tactile feedback.

Examples:

SERVICE CARD:

tap → card compresses slightly → expands into service details.

BOOK NOW:

button compresses → stretches → loading state → confirmation.

WORKER SELECTION:

worker card gently lifts and expands.

CHECKBOX:

small spring animation.

DATE SELECTION:

selected date pill morphs into active state.

STAR RATING:

stars pop in sequentially.

TOGGLE:

thumb uses spring physics.

NOTIFICATION:

badge pops in.

BOOKING CONFIRMATION:

subtle celebratory animation.

PAYMENT:

button transforms into loading → success state.

SERVICE COMPLETION:

timeline smoothly advances.

Use animation to communicate state changes, not simply to make things flashy.

================================================== 51. PREMIUM TYPOGRAPHY
Typography should feel editorial.

Use a modern high-quality sans-serif.

Strong hierarchy:

DISPLAY Large bold headlines

TITLE Bold section headings

BODY Comfortable readable text

CAPTION Small muted metadata

Avoid excessive font weights.

Use generous line-height.

Make important information extremely easy to scan.

================================================== 52. UI PHYSICALITY
Elements should feel like they have physical weight.

Use:

subtle shadows spring transitions soft corners layering depth micro scale changes

For example:

A bottom sheet should feel like it is physically sliding upward.

A button should feel like it can actually be pressed.

A worker marker should feel alive.

A notification should feel like it arrived.

A booking should feel like it transformed from a request into a real appointment.

This physicality is one of the defining characteristics of the demo. create a proper tokenized brand system so it doesn't randomly drift into purple/blue halfway through the app.

The visual DNA should basically be:

Vaishnavi posters → cream + forest green + terracotta → editorial photography → clean typography

Suhaeb interaction style → morphing → spring physics → stretchy buttons → bouncy cards → fluid transitions → tactile micro-interactions

Product inspiration → Urban Company service discovery → Rapido worker flow → Zomato live tracking → Instagram-style account switching

That combination is way more distinctive than just saying “make it modern.”

@tailwind base;
@tailwind components;
@tailwind utilities;

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   VAISHNAVI DESIGN TOKENS — Complete Brand System
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer base {
  :root {
    /* ── BRAND PALETTE ────────────────────────────────────── */
    --vaishnavi-green:        #173F35;
    --vaishnavi-green-dark:   #102F28;
    --vaishnavi-green-light:  #1E4D3F;
    --vaishnavi-green-pale:   rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.08);
    --vaishnavi-cream:        #F5F0E7;
    --vaishnavi-cream-deep:   #EDE8DD;
    --vaishnavi-cream-light:  #FBF9F4;
    --vaishnavi-terracotta:   #C86F52;
    --vaishnavi-terracotta-dark: #B55E42;
    --vaishnavi-terracotta-pale: rgba(200, 111, 82, 0.12);
    --vaishnavi-sage:         #A8B9A5;
    --vaishnavi-sage-light:   #C5D4C0;
    --vaishnavi-text:         #1A2E25;
    --vaishnavi-text-muted:   #5A6B5E;
    --vaishnavi-text-faint:   #8A9B8E;
    --vaishnavi-border:       rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.10);
    --vaishnavi-border-strong:rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.18);
    --vaishnavi-shadow-sm:    rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.06);
    --vaishnavi-shadow-md:    rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.10);
    --vaishnavi-shadow-lg:    rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.14);
    --vaishnavi-shadow-xl:    rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.18);
    --vaishnavi-white:        #FFFFFF;
    --vaishnavi-warm-gray:    #F0EBE1;
    --vaishnavi-bg-gradient:  linear-gradient(165deg, #FBF9F4 0%, #F5F0E7 50%, #EDE8DD 100%);
    --vaishnavi-card-gradient:linear-gradient(145deg, #FFFFFF 0%, #FEFCF8 100%);
    --vaishnavi-dark-gradient:linear-gradient(145deg, #173F35 0%, #102F28 100%);
    --vaishnavi-accent-gradient:linear-gradient(135deg, #C86F52 0%, #B55E42 100%);

    /* ── SHADOW SYSTEM (claymorphic) ──────────────────────── */
    --shadow-clay-sm:   0 1px 3px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-sm), 0 1px 2px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-sm);
    --shadow-clay-md:   0 4px 12px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-md), 0 2px 4px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-sm);
    --shadow-clay-lg:   0 8px 28px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-lg), 0 4px 8px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-md);
    --shadow-clay-xl:   0 16px 50px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-xl), 0 8px 16px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-lg);
    --shadow-clay-inner:inset 0 2px 4px var(--vaishnavi-shadow-sm), inset 0 -1px 2px rgba(23,63,53,0.04);
    --shadow-clay-float:0 20px 60px rgba(23,63,53,0.20), 0 8px 20px rgba(23,63,53,0.12);

    /* ── BORDER RADIUS ─────────────────────────────────────── */
    --radius-xs: 8px;
    --radius-sm: 12px;
    --radius-md: 16px;
    --radius-lg: 20px;
    --radius-xl: 24px;
    --radius-2xl: 28px;
    --radius-full: 9999px;

    /* ── TYPOGRAPHY ────────────────────────────────────────── */
    --font-display: 'Inter', 'Plus Jakarta Sans', system-ui, sans-serif;
    --font-body: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
    --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;

    /* ── SPACING SCALE ─────────────────────────────────────── */
    --space-xs:  4px;
    --space-sm:  8px;
    --space-md:  12px;
    --space-base:16px;
    --space-lg:  20px;
    --space-xl:  24px;
    --space-2xl: 32px;
    --space-3xl: 48px;
    --space-4xl: 64px;

    /* ── MOTION / SPRING CONFIG ────────────────────────────── */
    --spring-fast:   cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
    --spring-smooth: cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.24, 0.64, 1);
    --spring-bounce: cubic-bezier(0.68, -0.55, 0.27, 1.55);
    --ease-out-expo: cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    --ease-in-out:   cubic-bezier(0.65, 0, 0.35, 1);
    --trans-fast:    180ms var(--ease-out-expo);
    --trans-normal:  320ms var(--ease-out-expo);
    --trans-spring:  500ms var(--spring-smooth);
    --trans-slow:    600ms var(--ease-out-expo);

    /* ── DEPRECATED TAILWIND BACKWARDS COMPAT ─────────────── */
    --background: var(--vaishnavi-cream-light);
    --foreground: var(--vaishnavi-text);
    --card: #FFFFFF;
    --card-foreground: var(--vaishnavi-text);
    --primary: var(--vaishnavi-green);
    --primary-foreground: #FFFFFF;
    --secondary: var(--vaishnavi-cream);
    --secondary-foreground: var(--vaishnavi-green-dark);
    --muted: var(--vaishnavi-cream);
    --muted-foreground: var(--vaishnavi-text-muted);
    --accent: var(--vaishnavi-terracotta);
    --accent-foreground: #FFFFFF;
    --destructive: var(--vaishnavi-terracotta);
    --destructive-foreground: #FFFFFF;
    --border: var(--vaishnavi-border);
    --ring: var(--vaishnavi-green);
  }
}

@layer base {
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  html { scroll-behavior: smooth; }
  body {
    @apply bg-[var(--vaishnavi-cream-light)] text-[var(--vaishnavi-text)] antialiased;
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: 15px;
    line-height: 1.55;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
    overflow-x: hidden;
  }

  /* Subtle grain overlay via CSS pattern */
  body::before {
    content: '';
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 9999;
    opacity: 0.025;
    background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg viewBox='0 0 256 256' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%3E%3Cfilter id='n'%3E%3CfeTurbulence type='fractalNoise' baseFrequency='0.85' numOctaves='4' stitchTiles='stitch'/%3E%3C/filter%3E%3Crect width='100%25' height='100%25' filter='url(%23n)'/%3E%3C/svg%3E");
    background-repeat: repeat;
    background-size: 128px 128px;
  }

  h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: -0.025em;
    line-height: 1.2;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 5px; }
  ::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }
  ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--vaishnavi-sage); border-radius: 10px; }
  ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover { background: var(--vaishnavi-text-muted); }

  ::selection { background: var(--vaishnavi-terracotta-pale); color: var(--vaishnavi-green-dark); }

  input, textarea, select, button { font-family: inherit; }
}

@layer components {
  /* ── CLAYMORPHIC CARD BASE ─────────────────────────────── */
  .clay-card {
    background: var(--vaishnavi-card-gradient);
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    border: 1.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-clay-md),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9),
      inset 0 -1px 0 rgba(23,63,53,0.04);
    transition: box-shadow var(--trans-normal), transform var(--trans-spring);
  }
  .clay-card:active {
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-clay-sm),
      var(--shadow-clay-inner);
    transform: scale(0.985);
  }

  .clay-card-lg {
    background: var(--vaishnavi-card-gradient);
    border-radius: var(--radius-2xl);
    border: 1.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.85);
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-clay-lg),
      inset 0 2px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.95),
      inset 0 -1px 0 rgba(23,63,53,0.04);
    transition: box-shadow var(--trans-normal), transform var(--trans-spring);
  }
  .clay-card-lg:active {
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-md), var(--shadow-clay-inner);
    transform: scale(0.99);
  }

  /* ── SOFT CARD (cream variant) ─────────────────────────── */
  .clay-card-soft {
    background: linear-gradient(145deg, var(--vaishnavi-cream) 0%, var(--vaishnavi-cream-deep) 100%);
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    border: 1px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.06);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.6);
  }

  /* ── BUTTONS ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .btn-primary {
    background: var(--vaishnavi-dark-gradient);
    color: white;
    border: none;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 14px 28px;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 14px;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow:
      0 4px 14px rgba(23,63,53,0.30),
      0 1px 3px rgba(23,63,53,0.20),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.15);
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    letter-spacing: 0.01em;
  }
  .btn-primary::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.1) 0%, transparent 60%);
    border-radius: inherit;
  }
  .btn-primary:hover {
    transform: translateY(-1px);
    box-shadow:
      0 8px 24px rgba(23,63,53,0.35),
      0 2px 6px rgba(23,63,53,0.25),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.15);
  }
  .btn-primary:active {
    transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.97);
    box-shadow:
      0 2px 6px rgba(23,63,53,0.30),
      inset 0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
  }

  .btn-secondary {
    background: linear-gradient(145deg, var(--vaishnavi-cream) 0%, var(--vaishnavi-cream-deep) 100%);
    color: var(--vaishnavi-green);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--vaishnavi-border-strong);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 13px 26px;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 14px;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.7);
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
  }
  .btn-secondary:hover {
    transform: translateY(-1px);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-md), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.7);
    border-color: var(--vaishnavi-sage);
  }
  .btn-secondary:active {
    transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.97);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-inner);
  }

  .btn-accent {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--vaishnavi-terracotta) 0%, var(--vaishnavi-terracotta-dark) 100%);
    color: white;
    border: none;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 14px 28px;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 14px;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow:
      0 4px 14px rgba(200,111,82,0.35),
      0 1px 3px rgba(200,111,82,0.25),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
  }
  .btn-accent:hover {
    transform: translateY(-1px);
    box-shadow:
      0 8px 24px rgba(200,111,82,0.40),
      0 2px 6px rgba(200,111,82,0.30),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
  }
  .btn-accent:active {
    transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.97);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(200,111,82,0.30), inset 0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
  }

  .btn-ghost {
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--vaishnavi-green);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--vaishnavi-border);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 12px 24px;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 14px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
  }
  .btn-ghost:hover {
    background: var(--vaishnavi-green-pale);
    border-color: var(--vaishnavi-sage);
  }

  /* ── FOCUS RINGS ────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .focus-ring {
    outline: none;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(23,63,53,0.15);
  }
  .focus-ring:focus-visible {
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(23,63,53,0.25);
  }

  /* ── BADGES ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .badge {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 4px;
    padding: 4px 10px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-full);
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  }
  .badge-green { background: rgba(23,63,53,0.10); color: var(--vaishnavi-green); }
  .badge-orange { background: var(--vaishnavi-terracotta-pale); color: var(--vaishnavi-terracotta); }
  .badge-sage { background: rgba(168,185,165,0.20); color: #5A7A52; }
  .badge-white { background: white; color: var(--vaishnavi-green); box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm); }

  /* ── INPUT ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .input-field {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 13px 16px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--vaishnavi-border);
    background: white;
    color: var(--vaishnavi-text);
    font-size: 14px;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
    transition: border-color var(--trans-fast), box-shadow var(--trans-fast);
  }
  .input-field:focus {
    outline: none;
    border-color: var(--vaishnavi-green);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(23,63,53,0.12), var(--shadow-clay-sm);
  }
  .input-field::placeholder { color: var(--vaishnavi-text-faint); }

  /* ── AVATAR ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .avatar {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    border-radius: var(--radius-full);
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: 14px;
    color: white;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-md);
    background: linear-gradient(145deg, var(--vaishnavi-green) 0%, var(--vaishnavi-green-dark) 100%);
  }
  .avatar-sm { width: 32px; height: 32px; font-size: 12px; }
  .avatar-md { width: 40px; height: 40px; font-size: 14px; }
  .avatar-lg { width: 56px; height: 56px; font-size: 20px; }
  .avatar-xl { width: 80px; height: 80px; font-size: 28px; }

  /* ── SEPARATOR ──────────────────────────────────────────── */
  .sep { height: 1px; background: var(--vaishnavi-border); margin: 0; }
  .sep-v { width: 1px; background: var(--vaishnavi-border); margin: 0; }

  /* ── SECTION LABEL ──────────────────────────────────────── */
  .section-label {
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 0.18em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--vaishnavi-text-faint);
  }
}

@layer utilities {
  .scrollbar-hide { -ms-overflow-style: none; scrollbar-width: none; }
  .scrollbar-hide::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }

  .text-balance { text-wrap: balance; }

  /* Claymorphic depth layers */
  .depth-1 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm); }
  .depth-2 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-md); }
  .depth-3 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-lg); }
  .depth-4 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-xl); }
  .depth-float { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-float); }

  /* Gradient backgrounds */
  .bg-brand-dark { background: var(--vaishnavi-dark-gradient); }
  .bg-brand-accent { background: var(--vaishnavi-accent-gradient); }
  .bg-brand-cream { background: var(--vaishnavi-bg-gradient); }
}

&lt;/dyad-write&gt;

&lt;dyad-write path="src/components/icons.tsx" description=""&gt;
import { type SVGProps } from 'react';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CUSTOM VAISHNAVI ICONS — No library dependencies
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function IconBase({ children, size = 24, className = '' }: SVGProps&lt;SVGSVGElement&gt; &amp; { size?: number; className?: string }) {
  return (
    &lt;svg
      width={size}
      height={size}
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth={1.8}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      className={className}
    &gt;
      {children}
    &lt;/svg&gt;
  );
}

/* ── HOME ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* ── Service icon by key ─────────────────────────────────── */
export function getServiceIcon(serviceId: string, size = 28, color = 'currentColor') {
  const icon = SERVICE_ICONS[serviceId];
  if (!icon) return null;
  return (
    &lt;span style={{ color, width: size, height: size, display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}&gt;
      {icon}
    &lt;/span&gt;
  );
}

import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { getServiceIcon } from './icons';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SERVICE CARD — Claymorphic 3D card
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export function ServiceCard({ service, onPress, large = false }: { service: any; onPress?: () =&gt; void; large?: boolean }) {
  return (
    &lt;motion.button
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.92, rotate: large ? undefined : '-2deg' }}
      whileHover={{ y: -3, scale: 1.02 }}
      onClick={onPress}
      className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-2 min-w-[88px] ${large ? 'min-w-[110px]' : ''}`}
    &gt;
      &lt;div
        className="relative w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg overflow-hidden"
        style={{
          background: `linear-gradient(145deg, ${service.color}22 0%, ${service.color}11 100%)`,
          border: `1.5px solid ${service.color}30`,
          boxShadow: `0 4px 16px ${service.color}25, 0 2px 4px ${service.color}15, inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.5)`,
        }}
      &gt;
        {/* Inner highlight */}
        &lt;div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 h-1/2 rounded-t-2xl" style={{ background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)' }} /&gt;
        {getServiceIcon(service.id, 26, service.color)}
      &lt;/div&gt;
      &lt;span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#173F35] text-center leading-tight px-1" style={{ letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}&gt;
        {service.name}
      &lt;/span&gt;
    &lt;/motion.button&gt;
  );
}

@tailwind base;
@tailwind components;
@tailwind utilities;

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   VAISHNAVI DESIGN TOKENS — Complete Brand System
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer base {
  :root {
    /* ── BRAND PALETTE ────────────────────────────────────── */
    --va-green:        #173F35;
    --va-green-dark:   #102F28;
    --va-green-mid:    #1E4D3F;
    --va-green-pale:   rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.07);
    --va-green-soft:   rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.12);
    --va-cream:        #F5F0E7;
    --va-cream-deep:   #EDE8DD;
    --va-cream-light:  #FBF9F4;
    --va-warm-gray:    #F0EBE1;
    --va-terracotta:   #C86F52;
    --va-terra-dark:   #B55E42;
    --va-terra-pale:   rgba(200, 111, 82, 0.12);
    --va-sage:         #A8B9A5;
    --va-sage-light:   #C5D4C0;
    --va-sage-pale:    rgba(168, 185, 165, 0.20);
    --va-text:         #1A2E25;
    --va-text-muted:   #5A6B5E;
    --va-text-faint:   #8A9B8E;
    --va-border:       rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.09);
    --va-border-str:   rgba(23, 63, 53, 0.18);
    --va-white:        #FFFFFF;
    --va-page-bg:      #F5F0E7;
    --va-card-bg:      #FFFFFF;
    --va-card-cream:   #FAF8F3;
    --va-surface:      #F0EBE1;

    /* ── SHADOW SYSTEM (claymorphic) ──────────────────────── */
    --shadow-clay-xs:  0 1px 3px rgba(23,63,53,0.05), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
    --shadow-clay-sm:  0 2px 8px rgba(23,63,53,0.07), 0 1px 2px rgba(23,63,53,0.05), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9);
    --shadow-clay-md:  0 4px 16px rgba(23,63,53,0.09), 0 2px 4px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9);
    --shadow-clay-lg:  0 8px 28px rgba(23,63,53,0.11), 0 4px 8px rgba(23,63,53,0.07), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.95);
    --shadow-clay-xl:  0 16px 50px rgba(23,63,53,0.14), 0 8px 16px rgba(23,63,53,0.09), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,1);
    --shadow-clay-float:0 20px 60px rgba(23,63,53,0.18), 0 8px 20px rgba(23,63,53,0.10), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,1);
    --shadow-inset:    inset 0 2px 4px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 -1px 2px rgba(23,63,53,0.03);

    /* ── GREEN-SHADOWED variants for depth ────────────────── */
    --shadow-green-sm: 0 2px 8px rgba(23,63,53,0.18), 0 1px 2px rgba(23,63,53,0.12);
    --shadow-green-md: 0 4px 16px rgba(23,63,53,0.22), 0 2px 4px rgba(23,63,53,0.15);
    --shadow-green-lg: 0 8px 28px rgba(23,63,53,0.26), 0 4px 8px rgba(23,63,53,0.18);

    /* ── TERRACOTTA SHADOWS ───────────────────────────────── */
    --shadow-terra-sm: 0 2px 8px rgba(200,111,82,0.20), 0 1px 2px rgba(200,111,82,0.14);
    --shadow-terra-md: 0 4px 16px rgba(200,111,82,0.25), 0 2px 4px rgba(200,111,82,0.18);

    /* ── BORDER RADIUS SCALE ──────────────────────────────── */
    --radius-xs:  8px;
    --radius-sm:  12px;
    --radius-md:  16px;
    --radius-lg:  20px;
    --radius-xl:  24px;
    --radius-2xl: 28px;
    --radius-3xl: 32px;
    --radius-full: 9999px;

    /* ── TYPOGRAPHY ────────────────────────────────────────── */
    --font-display: 'Inter', 'Plus Jakarta Sans', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
    --font-body:    'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;

    /* ── MOTION / SPRING ───────────────────────────────────── */
    --spring-fast:   cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
    --spring-smooth: cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.24, 0.64, 1);
    --spring-bounce: cubic-bezier(0.68, -0.55, 0.27, 1.55);
    --ease-out-expo: cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    --ease-in-out:   cubic-bezier(0.65, 0, 0.35, 1);
    --trans-fast:    180ms var(--ease-out-expo);
    --trans-normal:  320ms var(--ease-out-expo);
    --trans-spring:  480ms var(--spring-smooth);
    --trans-slow:    600ms var(--ease-out-expo);

    /* ── LEGACY TAILWIND MAPPING ──────────────────────────── */
    --background: var(--va-cream-light);
    --foreground: var(--va-text);
    --card: var(--va-white);
    --card-foreground: var(--va-text);
    --popover: var(--va-white);
    --popover-foreground: var(--va-text);
    --primary: var(--va-green);
    --primary-foreground: #FFFFFF;
    --secondary: var(--va-cream);
    --secondary-foreground: var(--va-green-dark);
    --muted: var(--va-cream);
    --muted-foreground: var(--va-text-muted);
    --accent: var(--va-terracotta);
    --accent-foreground: #FFFFFF;
    --destructive: var(--va-terracotta);
    --destructive-foreground: #FFFFFF;
    --border: var(--va-border);
    --input: var(--va-border);
    --ring: var(--va-green);
    --radius: var(--radius-lg);
  }
}

/* ── IMPORT FONTS ────────────────────────────────────────── */
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@500;600;700;800&display=swap');

@layer base {
  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  html { scroll-behavior: smooth; -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent; }
  body {
    background: var(--va-cream-light);
    color: var(--va-text);
    font-family: var(--font-body);
    font-size: 15px;
    line-height: 1.55;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
    overflow-x: hidden;
  }

  /* Subtle film grain overlay — global, never interactive */
  body::before {
    content: '';
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 99999;
    opacity: 0.032;
    background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg viewBox='0 0 512 512' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%3E%3Cfilter id='noise'%3E%3CfeTurbulence type='fractalNoise' baseFrequency='0.82' numOctaves='4' stitchTiles='stitch'/%3E%3C/filter%3E%3Crect width='100%25' height='100%25' filter='url(%23noise)'/%3E%3C/svg%3E");
    background-repeat: repeat;
    background-size: 200px 200px;
    mix-blend-mode: multiply;
  }

  h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
    font-family: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: -0.028em;
    line-height: 1.15;
    color: var(--va-text);
  }

  ::selection { background: var(--va-terra-pale); color: var(--va-green-dark); }

  input, textarea, select, button, .input-field { font-family: var(--font-body); }

  /* Smooth focus rings */
  *:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--va-green);
    outline-offset: 2px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  }
}

@layer components {
  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     CLAYMORPHIC CARD SYSTEM
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .clay {
    background: linear-gradient(148deg, #FFFFFF 0%, #FEFCF8 60%, #FAF8F3 100%);
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    border: 1.5px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-clay-md),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.95);
    transition: box-shadow var(--trans-normal), transform var(--trans-spring);
  }
  .clay:hover {
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-lg), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.95);
  }
  .clay:active {
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm), var(--shadow-inset);
    transform: scale(0.988);
  }

  .clay-lg {
    background: linear-gradient(148deg, #FFFFFF 0%, #FEFCF8 50%, #FAF8F3 100%);
    border-radius: var(--radius-2xl);
    border: 1.5px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-clay-lg),
      inset 0 2px 0 rgba(255,255,255,1);
    transition: box-shadow var(--trans-normal), transform var(--trans-spring);
  }
  .clay-lg:active {
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-md), var(--shadow-inset);
    transform: scale(0.992);
  }

  .clay-soft {
    background: linear-gradient(148deg, var(--va-cream) 0%, var(--va-cream-deep) 100%);
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    border: 1px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.06);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.5);
  }

  .clay-green {
    background: linear-gradient(148deg, var(--va-green-mid) 0%, var(--va-green-dark) 100%);
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-green-md),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.12);
    color: #fff;
  }

  .clay-terra {
    background: linear-gradient(148deg, var(--va-terracotta) 0%, var(--va-terra-dark) 100%);
    border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12);
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-terra-md),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.18);
    color: #fff;
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     BUTTON SYSTEM — Tactile, springy
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .btn-primary {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 8px;
    background: linear-gradient(148deg, var(--va-green-mid) 0%, var(--va-green-dark) 100%);
    color: #FFFFFF;
    border: none;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 14px 28px;
    font-weight: 650;
    font-size: 14px;
    letter-spacing: 0.01em;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-green-md),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.14);
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
  }
  .btn-primary::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.08) 0%, transparent 55%);
    border-radius: inherit;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
  .btn-primary:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-green-lg), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.14);
  }
  .btn-primary:active {
    transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.97);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(23,63,53,0.35), var(--shadow-inset);
  }

  .btn-accent {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 8px;
    background: linear-gradient(148deg, var(--va-terracotta) 0%, var(--va-terra-dark) 100%);
    color: #FFFFFF;
    border: none;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 14px 28px;
    font-weight: 650;
    font-size: 14px;
    letter-spacing: 0.01em;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow:
      var(--shadow-terra-md),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.18);
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
  }
  .btn-accent::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.1) 0%, transparent 55%);
    border-radius: inherit;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
  .btn-accent:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(200,111,82,0.35), 0 2px 6px rgba(200,111,82,0.25), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.18);
  }
  .btn-accent:active {
    transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.97);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(200,111,82,0.30), var(--shadow-inset);
  }

  .btn-ghost {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 8px;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--va-green);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--va-border-str);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 12px 24px;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 14px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
  }
  .btn-ghost:hover {
    background: var(--va-green-pale);
    border-color: var(--va-sage);
    transform: translateY(-1px);
  }
  .btn-ghost:active {
    transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.97);
    background: var(--va-green-soft);
  }

  .btn-outline-terra {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 8px;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--va-terracotta);
    border: 1.5px solid rgba(200,111,82,0.35);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 12px 24px;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 14px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all var(--trans-fast);
  }
  .btn-outline-terra:hover {
    background: var(--va-terra-pale);
    border-color: var(--va-terracotta);
    transform: translateY(-1px);
  }
  .btn-outline-terra:active {
    transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.97);
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     INPUT & FORM ELEMENTS
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .input-field {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 13px 16px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--va-border);
    background: white;
    color: var(--va-text);
    font-size: 14px;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
    transition: border-color var(--trans-fast), box-shadow var(--trans-fast);
  }
  .input-field:focus {
    outline: none;
    border-color: var(--va-green);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(23,63,53,0.12), var(--shadow-clay-sm);
  }
  .input-field::placeholder { color: var(--va-text-faint); }

  .input-field--cream {
    background: var(--va-cream);
    border-color: rgba(23,63,53,0.07);
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     BADGE SYSTEM
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .badge {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 5px;
    padding: 4px 10px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-full);
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }
  .badge--green  { background: var(--va-green-pale); color: var(--va-green); }
  .badge--terra  { background: var(--va-terra-pale); color: var(--va-terracotta); }
  .badge--sage   { background: var(--va-sage-pale); color: #4A6A42; }
  .badge--white  { background: white; color: var(--va-green); box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-xs); }
  .badge--live   {
    background: rgba(23,63,53,0.10);
    color: var(--va-green);
    animation: badgePulse 2s ease-in-out infinite;
  }

  @keyframes badgePulse {
    0%, 100% { opacity: 1; }
    50% { opacity: 0.7; }
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     AVATAR
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .avatar {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    border-radius: var(--radius-full);
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #fff;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-md);
    background: linear-gradient(145deg, var(--va-green-mid) 0%, var(--va-green-dark) 100%);
  }
  .avatar--xs { width: 28px; height: 28px; font-size: 11px; }
  .avatar--sm { width: 36px; height: 36px; font-size: 13px; }
  .avatar--md { width: 44px; height: 44px; font-size: 16px; }
  .avatar--lg { width: 60px; height: 60px; font-size: 22px; }
  .avatar--xl { width: 80px; height: 80px; font-size: 28px; }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION LABELS
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .section-label {
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: 0.18em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--va-text-faint);
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     NAV DIVIDER
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .nav-sep { height: 1px; background: var(--va-border); margin: 0; }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SCROLLBAR HIDE
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .scrollbar-hide { -ms-overflow-style: none; scrollbar-width: none; }
  .scrollbar-hide::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     DEPTH LAYERS
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .depth-1 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-sm); }
  .depth-2 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-md); }
  .depth-3 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-lg); }
  .depth-4 { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-xl); }
  .depth-float { box-shadow: var(--shadow-clay-float); }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     GLASS (for overlays/maps only)
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  .glass {
    background: rgba(250, 248, 243, 0.82);
    backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(160%);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(160%);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5);
    box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(23,63,53,0.12);
  }

  .glass-dark {
    background: rgba(16, 47, 40, 0.85);
    backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(180%);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(180%);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
    box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    color: white;
  }
}

@layer utilities {
  .text-balance { text-wrap: balance; }
  .preserve-3d { transform-style: preserve-3d; }
  .perspective-1000 { perspective: 1000px; }

  /* Gradient backgrounds */
  .bg-brand-dark  { background: linear-gradient(148deg, var(--va-green-mid) 0%, var(--va-green-dark) 100%); }
  .bg-brand-cream { background: linear-gradient(165deg, var(--va-cream-light) 0%, var(--va-cream) 50%, var(--va-cream-deep) 100%); }
}

</dyad-write>

<dyad-write path="src/components/icons.tsx" description="">
import { type SVGProps } from 'react';

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   VAISHNAVI CUSTOM ICON SYSTEM
   Hand-crafted SVG icons — zero external icon library deps
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

type IconProps = SVGProps<SVGSVGElement> & { size?: number; className?: string };

const BaseIcon = ({ children, size = 24, className = '' }: IconProps) => (
  <svg
    width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
    stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
    className={className} aria-hidden="true"
  >{children}</svg>
);

/* ── Navigation ───────────────────────────────────────────── */
export const IconHome = (p: IconProps) => (
  <BaseIcon {...p}>
    <path d="M3 10.5L12 3l9 7.5V20a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H4a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V10.5z"/>
    <polyline points="9 22 9 12 15 12 15 22"/>
  </BaseIcon>
);

);

    <path d="M19.4 15a1.65 1.65 0 0 0 .33 1.82l.06.06a2 2 0 0 1-2.83 2.83l-.06-.06a1.65 1.65 0 0 0-1.82-.33 1.65 1.65 0 0 0-1 1.51V21a2 2 0 0 1-4 0v-.09A1.65 1.65 0 0 0 9 19.4a1.65 1.65 0 0 0-1.82.33l-.06.06a2 2 0 0 1-2.83-2.83l.06-.06A1.65 1.65 0 0 0 4.68 15a1.65 1.65 0 0 0-1.51-1H3a2 2 0 0 1 0-4h.09A1.65 1.65 0 0 0 4.6 9a1.65 1.65 0 0 0-.33-1.82l-.06-.06a2 2 0 0 1 2.83-2.83l.06.06A1.65 1.65 0 0 0 9 4.68a1.65 1.65 0 0 0 1-1.51V3a2 2 0 0 1 4 0v.09a1.65 1.65 0 0 0 1 1.51 1.65 1.65 0 0 0 1.82-.33l.06-.06a2 2 0 0 1 2.83 2.83l-.06.06A1.65 1.65 0 0 0 19.4 9a1.65 1.65 0 0 0 1.51 1H21a2 2 0 0 1 0 4h-.09a1.65 1.65 0 0 0-1.51 1z"/>
  </BaseIcon>
);

export const IconMoreHorizontal = (p: IconProps) => (
  <BaseIcon {...p}>
    <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="1"/>
    <circle cx="19" cy="12" r="1"/>
    <circle cx="5" cy="12" r="1"/>
  </BaseIcon>
);

export const IconMinus = (p: IconProps) => (
  <BaseIcon {...p}>
    <line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/>
  </BaseIcon>
);

export const IconPause = (p: IconProps) => (
  <BaseIcon {...p}>
    <rect x="6" y="4" width="4" height="16"/>
    <rect x="14" y="4" width="4" height="16"/>
  </BaseIcon>
);

export const IconPlay = (p: IconProps) => (
  <BaseIcon {...p}>
    <polygon points="5 3 19 12 5 21 5 3"/>
  </BaseIcon>
);

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SERVICE ICONS — Custom SVG per service type
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

const SERVICE_SVG: Record<string, React.ReactNode> = {
  housekeeping: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <rect x="3" y="8" width="22" height="16" rx="2" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"/>
      <line x1="3" y1="14" x2="25" y2="14" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4"/>
      <path d="M9 8V5a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h8a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v3" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4"/>
      <circle cx="14" cy="19" r="1.5" fill="currentColor"/>
    </svg>
  ),
  cooking: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <path d="M5 20c0-4 3-8 9-10 6 2 9 6 9 10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round"/>
      <path d="M8 20h12" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4"/>
      <path d="M11 10c0-2 1-4 3-4s3 2 3 4" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4"/>
      <path d="M7 8c.5-1 1.5-2 2-3" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinecap="round"/>
      <path d="M14 5c.5-1 1.5-2 2-3" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinecap="round"/>
      <path d="M21 8c.5-1 1.5-2 2-3" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2" strokeLinecap="round"/>
    </svg>
  ),
  plumbing: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <path d="M19 4l-8 8" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round"/>
      <path d="M15 4h4v4" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"/>
      <path d="M5 19l4-4" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round"/>
      <rect x="14" y="14" width="10" height="10" rx="2" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"/>
      <circle cx="19" cy="19" r="2" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5"/>
    </svg>
  ),
  electrical: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <polygon points="16 3 6 16 13 16 11 25 22 11 15 11 16 3" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinejoin="round"/>
    </svg>
  ),
  security: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <path d="M14 3L4 8v7c0 6 4 10 10 12 6-2 10-6 10-12V8L14 3z" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"/>
      <polyline points="10 14 13 17 18 11" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"/>
    </svg>
  ),
  elder_care: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <path d="M14 24s-7-4.5-7-9.5c0-3.5 3-6 7-6s7 2.5 7 6c0 5-7 9.5-7 9.5z" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"/>
      <circle cx="11" cy="13" r="1.5" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>
      <circle cx="17" cy="13" r="1.5" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>
      <path d="M12 17c.8.8 2.2.8 3 0" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round"/>
    </svg>
  ),
  caretaker: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <rect x="4" y="10" width="20" height="14" rx="2" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"/>
      <path d="M9 10V7a5 5 0 0 1 10 0v3" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"/>
      <circle cx="14" cy="17" r="2" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5"/>
    </svg>
  ),
  home_support: (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none" className="w-full h-full">
      <path d="M14 3L3 12v11a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h18a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V12L14 3z" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7"/>
      <rect x="10" y="17" width="8" height="8" rx="1" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5"/>
      <line x1="14" y1="17" x2="14" y2="25" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5"/>
    </svg>
  ),
};

export function ServiceIcon({ serviceId, size = 28, color = 'currentColor' }: { serviceId: string; size?: number; color?: string }) {
  const svg = SERVICE_SVG[serviceId];
  if (!svg) return null;
  return (
    <span style={{ color, width: size, height: size, display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
      {svg}
    </span>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   STAR RATING COMPONENT
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function StarRating({ rating, size = 14, count = 5 }: { rating: number; size?: number; count?: number }) {
  return (
    <span className="flex items-center gap-0.5" aria-label={`Rating: ${rating} out of 5`}>
      {Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, i) => (
        <IconStar
          key={i}
          size={size}
          filled={i < Math.round(rating)}
          className={i < Math.round(rating) ? 'text-[#C86F52]' : 'text-[#D4CFC4]'}
        />
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EXPORT ALL ICONS AS NAMED EXPORTS FOR CONVENIENCE
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export default {
  IconHome, IconSearch, IconCalendar, IconBell, IconUser, IconChat,
  IconArrowLeft, IconArrowRight, IconCheck, IconX, IconMapPin,
  IconPhone, IconClock, IconStar, IconTrendingUp, IconWallet,
  IconBriefcase, IconLayout, IconMap, IconClipboard, IconUsers,
  IconNav, IconTruck, IconLiveTrack, IconVerified, IconSend,
  IconPlus, IconMinus, IconMic, IconSpeaker, IconLogOut,
  IconHelpCircle, IconCreditCard, IconHeartOutline, IconWifi,
  IconWifiOff, IconShield, IconWrench, IconLightning, IconSparkle,
  IconHeart, IconChef, IconBroom, IconSettings, IconMoreHorizontal,
  IconPause, IconPlay,
};

<dyad-write path="src/components/Cards.tsx" description="">
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { ServiceIcon, StarRating } from './icons';

const SPRING = { type: 'spring', damping: 18, stiffness: 300 };
const FADE_UP = { initial: { opacity: 0, y: 12 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 }, transition: SPRING };

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SERVICE CARD — Small circular icon card (horizontal scroll)
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function ServiceCard({ service, onPress, large = false }: { service: any; onPress?: () => void; large?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <motion.button
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.88, rotate: large ? undefined : '-4deg' }}
      whileHover={{ y: -4, scale: 1.04 }}
      onClick={onPress}
      className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-2 min-w-[82px] ${large ? 'min-w-[105px]' : ''}`}
      {...FADE_UP}
    >
      <div
        className="relative w-16 h-16 rounded-[22px] flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden shadow-md"
        style={{
          background: `linear-gradient(145deg, ${service.color}20 0%, ${service.color}08 100%)`,
          border: `1.5px solid ${service.color}28`,
          boxShadow: `0 4px 16px ${service.color}28, 0 2px 4px ${service.color}16, inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.5)`,
        }}
      >
        {/* Top highlight */}
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 h-[45%] rounded-t-[20px]" style={{ background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.20)' }} />
        <div className="relative z-10" style={{ color: service.color }}>
          <ServiceIcon serviceId={service.id} size={28} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#173F35] text-center leading-tight px-1" style={{ letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}>
        {service.name}
      </span>
    </motion.button>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SERVICE CARD — Large featured horizontal card
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function ServiceCardLarge({ service, onPress, gradient }: { service: any; onPress?: () => void; gradient?: string }) {
  return (
    <motion.button
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.97, y: 2 }}
      whileHover={{ y: -3, boxShadow: '0 10px 32px rgba(23,63,53,0.18)' }}
      onClick={onPress}
      className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-4 rounded-[22px]"
      style={{
        background: 'white',
        border: '1.5px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.07)',
        boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="w-14 h-14 rounded-[18px] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
        style={{
          background: `linear-gradient(145deg, ${service.color}1e 0%, ${service.color}0a 100%)`,
          border: `1.5px solid ${service.color}22`,
          boxShadow: `0 3px 12px ${service.color}22`,
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ color: service.color }}><ServiceIcon serviceId={service.id} size={26} /></div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 text-left min-w-0">
        <p className="font-extrabold text-sm text-[#173F35]" style={{ letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}>{service.name}</p>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-[#7A8B7E] mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">{service.description.slice(0, 48)}…</p>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1.5">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#C86F52] bg-[#C86F52]/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
            {service.options.length} options
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <IconArrowRight size={18} className="text-[#A8B9A5] shrink-0" />
    </motion.button>
  );
}

function IconArrowRight({ size = 24, className = '' }: { size?: number; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className={className}>
      <line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/><polyline points="12 5 19 12 12 19"/>
    </svg>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   BOOKING CARD — Compact booking summary card
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function BookingCard({ booking, worker, onPress }: { booking: any; worker?: any; onPress?: () => void }) {
  const statusColors: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
    requested:     { bg: 'rgba(200,111,82,0.12)',  text: '#B55E42' },
    assigned:      { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    on_the_way:    { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    nearby:        { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    arrived:       { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    in_progress:   { bg: 'rgba(200,111,82,0.12)',  text: '#C86F52' },
    completed:     { bg: 'rgba(168,185,165,0.20)', text: '#4A6A42' },
    paid:          { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    reviewed:      { bg: 'rgba(168,185,165,0.20)', text: '#4A6A42' },
  };
  const sc = statusColors[booking.status] || statusColors.completed;
  const svc = booking.serviceType?.replace(/_/g, ' ');

  return (
    <motion.button
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.98, y: 1 }}
      onClick={onPress}
      className="w-full text-left rounded-[20px] p-4"
      style={{
        background: 'white',
        border: '1.5px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.07)',
        boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
      }}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div
            className="w-11 h-11 rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
            style={{ background: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.07)' }}
          >
            <span style={{ fontSize: 20 }}>{booking.serviceType === 'plumbing' ? '🔧' : booking.serviceType === 'cooking' ? '👩🍳' : booking.serviceType === 'housekeeping' ? '🧹' : booking.serviceType === 'electrical' ? '⚡' : booking.serviceType === 'security' ? '🛡️' : '🏠'}</span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="font-bold text-[#173F35] text-sm capitalize">{svc}</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-[#7A8B7E] mt-0.5">{worker?.name || 'Professional'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span
          className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full capitalize"
          style={{ background: sc.bg, color: sc.text }}
        >
          {booking.status === 'on_the_way' ? '● LIVE' : booking.status.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-[#7A8B7E]">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">📅 {booking.date}</span>
          <span>·</span>
          <span>{booking.time}</span>
        </div>
        <span className="font-extrabold text-[#173F35] text-sm">₹{booking.estimatedPrice?.toLocaleString()}</span>
      </div>
    </motion.button>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   WORKER CARD — Selection card for worker picker
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function WorkerCard({ worker, selected, onPress }: { worker: any; selected?: boolean; onPress?: () => void }) {
  return (
    <motion.button
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}
      whileHover={{ y: -2 }}
      onClick={onPress}
      className="w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 p-3.5 rounded-[20px]"
      style={{
        background: selected
          ? 'linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(23,63,53,0.08) 0%, rgba(23,63,53,0.04) 100%)'
          : 'white',
        border: selected ? '2px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.35)' : '1.5px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.07)',
        boxShadow: selected
          ? '0 4px 16px rgba(23,63,53,0.14), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.8)'
          : '0 2px 8px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
      }}
    >
      {/* Avatar with ring */}
      <div className="relative shrink-0">
        <div
          className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-base"
          style={{
            background: 'linear-gradient(145deg, #1E4D3F 0%, #102F28 100%)',
            boxShadow: '0 3px 10px rgba(23,63,53,0.30)',
          }}
        >
          {worker.name.charAt(0)}
        </div>
        {selected && (
          <div className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-[#C86F52] flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
            <svg width="9" height="9" viewBox="0 0 10 8" fill="none"><path d="M1 4L3.5 6.5L9 1.5" stroke="white" strokeWidth="1.8" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"/></svg>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 text-left min-w-0">
        <p className="font-bold text-[#173F35] text-sm truncate">{worker.name}</p>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-[#7A8B7E] mt-0.3 capitalize">{worker.serviceType.replace(/_/g, ' ')} Professional</p>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-1">
          <StarRating rating={worker.rating} size={12} />
          <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#173F35] ml-0.5">{worker.rating}</span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-[#A8B9A5]">· {worker.totalJobs} jobs</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {worker.status === 'online' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1 shrink-0">
          <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-green-500 shadow-sm" style={{ boxShadow: '0 0 6px rgba(34,197,94,0.6)' }} />
          <span className="text-[9px] font-semibold text-[#4A6A42]">Online</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </motion.button>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   STATUS BADGE
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function StatusBadge({ status, small = false }: { status: string; small?: boolean }) {
  const map: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; dot?: boolean }> = {
    requested:    { bg: 'rgba(200,111,82,0.12)',  text: '#B55E42' },
    assigned:     { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    on_the_way:   { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35', dot: true },
    nearby:       { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    arrived:      { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    in_progress:  { bg: 'rgba(200,111,82,0.12)',  text: '#C86F52' },
    completed:    { bg: 'rgba(168,185,165,0.20)', text: '#4A6A42' },
    paid:         { bg: 'rgba(23,63,53,0.10)',    text: '#173F35' },
    reviewed:     { bg: 'rgba(168,185,165,0.20)', text: '#4A6A42' },
  };
  const s = map[status] || map.completed;
  const padding = small ? '3px 8px' : '4px 10px';
  const fontSize = small ? '10px' : '11px';

  return (
    <span
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold tracking-wide"
      style={{ background: s.bg, color: s.text, padding, borderRadius: 9999, fontSize, textTransform: 'capitalize' }}
    >
      {s.dot && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-pulse" />}
      {status.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
    </span>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   STAT CARD — for dashboards
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function StatCard({ label, value, sub, icon, color, onPress }: {
  label: string; value: string; sub?: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode; color: string; onPress?: () => void;
}) {
  const Btn = onPress ? 'button' : 'div';
  return (
    <motion.div
      as={Btn}
      whileTap={onPress ? { scale: 0.96, y: 2 } : undefined}
      className="rounded-[20px] p-4"
      style={{
        background: 'white',
        border: '1.5px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.07)',
        boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="w-9 h-9 rounded-[12px] flex items-center justify-center mb-3"
        style={{ background: color + '14', color }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>
      <p className="text-[22px] font-extrabold text-[#173F35] leading-none" style={{ letterSpacing: '-0.03em' }}>{value}</p>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-[#7A8B7E] font-medium mt-1">{label}</p>
      {sub && <p className="text-[10px] text-[#A8B9A5] mt-0.5">{sub}</p>}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DATE PILLS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function DatePill({ date, label, selected, onPress }: { date: string; label: string; selected?: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
  const isToday = label === 'Today';
  const isTomorrow = label === 'Tomorrow';
  return (
    <motion.button
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.93 }}
      onClick={onPress}
      className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 px-4 py-3 rounded-[18px] min-w-[68px]"
      style={{
        background: selected
          ? 'linear-gradient(145deg, #1E4D3F 0%, #102F28 100%)'
          : 'white',
        border: selected ? 'none' : '1.5px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.08)',
        boxShadow: selected
          ? '0 4px 14px rgba(23,63,53,0.30), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'
          : '0 2px 6px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
        color: selected ? 'white' : '#173F35',
      }}
    >
      <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold opacity-70">{isToday ? 'TODAY' : isTomorrow ? 'TOM' : date.split('-')[2]}</span>
      <span className="text-[13px] font-extrabold">{label.split(' ')[0]}</span>
      {selected && <div className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#C86F52]" style={{ boxShadow: '0 0 4px rgba(200,111,82,0.8)' }} />}
    </motion.button>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TIME PILLS
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export function TimePill({ time, selected, onPress }: { time: string; selected?: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
  return (
    <motion.button
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.93 }}
      onClick={onPress}
      className="px-5 py-3 rounded-[16px] font-semibold text-sm"
      style={{
        background: selected
          ? 'linear-gradient(145deg, #C86F52 0%, #B55E42 100%)'
          : 'white',
        border: selected ? 'none' : '1.5px solid rgba(23,63,53,0.08)',
        boxShadow: selected
          ? '0 4px 14px rgba(200,111,82,0.30), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.2)'
          : '0 2px 6px rgba(23,63,53,0.06), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
        color: selected ? 'white' : '#173F35',
      }}
    >
      {time}
    </motion.button>
  );
}

</dyad-write>

  { id: 'n7', userId: 'c4', type: 'payment', title: 'Payment Received', message: 'Payment of ₹1,200 received for security guard booking.', reading: true, createdAt: ago(8_000_000_000) },
  { id: 'n8', userId: 'w6', type: 'new_booking', title: 'New Request', message: 'Meera Sharma requested lunch/dinner cooking for Sep 15.', reading: false, createdAt: ago(180000) },
  { id: 'n9', userId: 'c1', type: 'rating', title: 'Rate Your Experience', message: 'How was your cooking session with Priya? Leave a review!', reading: true, createdAt: ago(1_800_000_000) },
  { id: 'n10', userId: 'admin1', type: 'revenue', title: 'Daily Revenue', message: 'Today\'s revenue: ₹18,450 across 24 bookings.', reading: true, createdAt: ago(3600000) },
];

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   BOOKINGS — Full lifecycle with historical data
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function generateAllBookings(): (Booking | BookingEvent)[] {
  const items: (Booking | BookingEvent)[] = [];
  const hyDates = ['2025-09-01','2025-09-03','2025-09-05','2025-09-07','2025-09-09',
                   '2025-09-10','2025-09-12','2025-09-14','2025-09-16'];
  const hyTimes = ['10:00 AM','11:00 AM','12:00 PM','1:00 PM','2:00 PM','3:00 PM','4:00 PM','5:00 PM','6:00 PM'];
  const svcTypes: ServiceType[] = ['plumbing','housekeeping','cooking','electrical','elder_care','security','cooking','plumbing','housekeeping'];
  const statuses: BookingStatus[] = ['reviewed','completed','paid','completed','completed','paid','in_progress','requested','completed'];
  const prices   = [850, 699, 450, 600, 1200, 800, 550, 650, 750];

  hyDates.forEach((date, i) => {
    const cust = CUSTOMERS[i % CUSTOMERS.length];
    const svc = svcTypes[i];
    const worker = WORKERS.find(w => w.serviceType === svc) || WORKERS[0];
    const bid = `vh-${['past-0','past-1','past-2','past-3','past-4','cook-1','elec-1','plumb-2','house-2'][i]}`;
    const st = statuses[i];
    const created = ago((i + 1) * 2_500_000_000);

    const booking: Booking = {
      id: bid, customerId: cust.id, workerId: worker.id, serviceType: svc,
      serviceOptionId: 'general', addressId: cust.defaultAddress?.id || 'a1',
      date, time: hyTimes[i], status: st, estimatedPrice: prices[i],
      finalPrice: st === 'completed' || st === 'paid' ? prices[i] : undefined,
      createdAt: created,
    };
    items.push(booking);
    items.push({ id: `${bid}-evt-create`, bookingId: bid, timestamp: created, actorId: cust.id, actorRole: 'customer', type: 'created', message: `Booking created for ${svc}` });
    if (st !== 'requested') {
      items.push({ id: `${bid}-evt-assign`, bookingId: bid, timestamp: created + 60000, actorId: worker.id, actorRole: 'worker', type: 'assigned', message: 'Professional assigned' });
    }
    if (st === 'in_progress' || st === 'completed' || st === 'paid' || st === 'reviewed') {
      items.push({ id: `${bid}-evt-start`, bookingId: bid, timestamp: created + 120000, actorId: worker.id, actorRole: 'worker', type: 'started', message: 'Service started' });
    }
    if (st === 'completed' || st === 'paid' || st === 'reviewed') {
      items.push({ id: `${bid}-evt-comp`, bookingId: bid, timestamp: created + 300000, actorId: worker.id, actorRole: 'worker', type: 'completed', message: 'Service completed' });
    }
    if (st === 'paid' || st === 'reviewed') {
      items.push({ id: `${bid}-evt-paid`, bookingId: bid, timestamp: created + 360000, actorId: cust.id, actorRole: 'customer', type: 'paid', message: `Payment of ₹${prices[i]} received` });
    }
  });

  // ── Active / upcoming bookings ───────────────────────────
  const tomorrow = new Date(Date.now() + 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const dayAfter = new Date(Date.now() + 172800000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const laterThisWeek = new Date(Date.now() + 432000000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  // Suhaeb's active plumbing (requested → the demo hook)
  const vh2048: Booking = {
    id: 'vh-2048', customerId: 'c1', workerId: 'w1', serviceType: 'plumbing',
    serviceOptionId: 'bathroom_issue', addressId: 'a1',
    date: tomorrow, time: '4:00 PM', status: 'requested',
    estimatedPrice: 650, createdAt: ago(600000),
  };
  items.push(vh2048);
  items.push({ id: 'vh-2048-e1', bookingId: 'vh-2048', timestamp: ago(600000), actorId: 'c1', actorRole: 'customer', type: 'created', message: 'Booking requested' });

  // Meera's housekeeping (in_progress — shows real activity)
  const vhHouseActive: Booking = {
    id: 'vh-house-active', customerId: 'c2', workerId: 'w5', serviceType: 'housekeeping',
    serviceOptionId: 'full_home', addressId: 'a3',
    date: todayStr(), time: '11:00 AM', status: 'in_progress',
    estimatedPrice: 999, createdAt: ago(7_200_000),
  };
  items.push(vhHouseActive);
  items.push({ id: 'vh-ha-e1', bookingId: 'vh-house-active', timestamp: ago(7_200_000), actorId: 'c2', actorRole: 'customer', type: 'created', message: 'Booking requested' });
  items.push({ id: 'vh-ha-e2', bookingId: 'vh-house-active', timestamp: ago(6_900_000), actorId: 'w5', actorRole: 'worker', type: 'accepted', message: 'Rahul accepted the request' });
  items.push({ id: 'vh-ha-e3', bookingId: 'vh-house-active', timestamp: ago(6_000_000), actorId: 'w5', actorRole: 'worker', type: 'started', message: 'Service in progress' });

  // Ananya's cooking (on_the_way)
  const vhCookOnWay: Booking = {
    id: 'vh-cook-onway', customerId: 'c4', workerId: 'w12', serviceType: 'cooking',
    serviceOptionId: 'special_meal', addressId: 'a5',
    date: todayStr(), time: '1:00 PM', status: 'on_the_way',
    estimatedPrice: 1200, createdAt: ago(3_600_000),
  };
  items.push(vhCookOnWay);
  items.push({ id: 'vh-cow-e1', bookingId: 'vh-cook-onway', timestamp: ago(3_600_000), actorId: 'c4', actorRole: 'customer', type: 'created', message: 'Booking requested' });
  items.push({ id: 'vh-cow-e2', bookingId: 'vh-cook-onway', timestamp: ago(3_300_000), actorId: 'w12', actorRole: 'worker', type: 'accepted', message: 'Fatima accepted the request' });
  items.push({ id: 'vh-cow-e3', bookingId: 'vh-cook-onway', timestamp: ago(1_800_000), actorId: 'w12', actorRole: 'worker', type: 'on_the_way', message: 'Fatima is on the way' });

  // Ravi's electrical (assigned)
  const vhElecAssign: Booking = {
    id: 'vh-elec-asgn', customerId: 'c3', workerId: 'w4', serviceType: 'electrical',
    serviceOptionId: 'fan_light', addressId: 'a4',
    date: tomorrow, time: '10:00 AM', status: 'assigned',
    estimatedPrice: 550, createdAt: ago(1_800_000),
  };
  items.push(vhElecAssign);
  items.push({ id: 'vh-ea-e1', bookingId: 'vh-elec-asgn', timestamp: ago(1_800_000), actorId: 'c3', actorRole: 'customer', type: 'created', message: 'Booking requested' });
  items.push({ id: 'vh-ea-e2', bookingId: 'vh-elec-asgn', timestamp: ago(1_500_000), actorId: 'w4', actorRole: 'worker', type: 'accepted', message: 'Arjun accepted the request' });

  // Ananya's elder care (upcoming tomorrow)
  const vhElderUp: Booking = {
    id: 'vh-elder-up', customerId: 'c4', workerId: 'w8', serviceType: 'elder_care',
    serviceOptionId: 'companion', addressId: 'a5',
    date: tomorrow, time: '9:00 AM', status: 'requested',
    estimatedPrice: 800, createdAt: ago(900000),
  };
  items.push(vhElderUp);
  items.push({ id: 'vh-eu-e1', bookingId: 'vh-elder-up', timestamp: ago(900000), actorId: 'c4', actorRole: 'customer', type: 'created', message: 'Booking requested' });

  return items;
}

function todayStr() { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SEED FUNCTION
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
export async function seedDatabase() {
  try {
    const userCount = await db.users.count();
    if (userCount > 0) return; // already seeded

    const allUsers: User[] = [
      ...CUSTOMERS.map(c => ({ ...c, role: 'customer' as const })),
      ...WORKERS.map(w => ({ ...w, role: 'worker' as const })),
      ...ADMINS.map(a => ({ ...a, role: 'admin' as const })),
    ];

    await db.transaction('rw', db.users, db.workers, db.customers, db.admins, async () => {
      await db.users.bulkAdd(allUsers);
      await db.workers.bulkAdd(WORKERS);
      await db.customers.bulkAdd(CUSTOMERS);
      await db.admins.bulkAdd(ADMINS);
    });

    await db.transaction('rw', db.services, db.addresses, db.ads, async () => {
      await db.services.bulkAdd(SERVICES);
      await db.addresses.bulkAdd(ADDRESSES);
      await db.ads.bulkAdd(ADS);
    });

    const bae = generateAllBookings();
    const bk = bae.filter(x => 'customerId' in x) as Booking[];
    const ev = bae.filter(x => 'type' in x && 'message' in x) as BookingEvent[];

    await db.transaction('rw', db.bookings, db.bookingEvents, db.reviews, async () => {
      await db.bookings.bulkAdd(bk);
      await db.bookingEvents.bulkAdd(ev);
      await db.reviews.bulkAdd(REVIEWS);
    });

    await db.transaction('rw', db.payments, db.messages, db.notifications, async () => {
      await db.payments.bulkAdd(PAYMENTS);
      await db.messages.bulkAdd(MESSAGES);
      await db.notifications.bulkAdd(NOTIFICATIONS);
    });

    console.log('[Seed] Database initialized with', bk.length, 'bookings,', WORKERS.length, 'workers,', CUSTOMERS.length, 'customers');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[DB Seed Error]', e);
  }
}
